--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

================================================================================
  METHODOLOGY
================================================================================
  WIN/LOSS  : TP or SL hit — no time limit — ONGOING if still open
  MA44      : Simple SMA(44) computed bar by bar
  ATR       : Wilder smoothing, period 14 | Floor 0.040% | Ceil 0.120%
  4H gate   : MA44(44) on H4 candles — cached per 4h bucket
  Outcome   : SL/TP checked candle by candle from bar after entry
  Data      : OANDA fxPractice mid prices (bid/ask average)
================================================================================
